W4-08happy_pathcompletedTrue00
Review output and evidence
{
  "id": "W4-08",
  "scenario": "happy_path",
  "title": "Snapshot equality and stale threshold",
  "failure_mode": "Strict future comparison versus inclusive age comparison",
  "source": {
    "path": "triage_desk/freshness.py",
    "start_line": 7,
    "end_line": 30,
    "sha256": "48adfd3357bd0c1dc803fb1cae1e69f5c69ca529f3bbaaef4579e67e18e01b91",
    "quote": "def corpus_freshness(sources: list[dict], *, now: datetime | None = None, max_age_days: int = 7) -> dict:\n    if not isinstance(max_age_days, int) or isinstance(max_age_days, bool) or max_age_days < 1:\n        raise ValueError('Maximum snapshot age must be a positive number of days')\n    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    if now.tzinfo is None:\n        raise ValueError('Snapshot age requires a timezone-aware current time')\n    snapshots = []\n    missing = 0\n    for source in sources:\n        try:\n            snapshot = datetime.fromisoformat(str(source.get('snapshot_at', '')).replace('Z', '+00:00'))\n            if snapshot.tzinfo is None or snapshot > now:\n                raise ValueError('Invalid snapshot time')\n            snapshots.append(snapshot)\n        except (ValueError, TypeError):\n            missing += 1\n    oldest = min(snapshots) if snapshots else None\n    age_days = (now - oldest).total_seconds() / 86400 if oldest else None\n    status = 'unknown' if missing or not oldest else 'stale' if age_days >= max_age_days else 'current'\n    return {'status': status, 'oldest_snapshot_at': oldest.isoformat() if oldest else None,\n            'age_days': round(age_days, 2) if age_days is not None else None,\n            'max_age_days': max_age_days, 'missing_or_invalid_timestamps': missing,\n            'refresh_mode': 'manual',\n            'note': 'Snapshot age measures fetch time, not whether an upstream claim or version is still correct.'}\n"
  },
  "context": {
    "question": "Statically review the selected evidence. Separate supported observations from unverified outcomes and propose focused checks.",
    "reported_version": ""
  },
  "label_review": {
    "status": "pending",
    "reviewer": null,
    "notes": ""
  },
  "annotations": {
    "findings": [
      {
        "statement": "The snapshot > now check permits equality with now; with no invalid snapshots, stale begins when age_days is at least max_age_days.",
        "expected_supported": true,
        "rationale": "The future comparison is strict while the stale comparison is inclusive."
      },
      {
        "statement": "A snapshot exactly equal to now is rejected as a future timestamp.",
        "expected_supported": false,
        "rationale": "Equality does not satisfy snapshot > now."
